# March
Source: https://docs.qonto.com/qonto-embed/product-updates/2025/march



## ✈️  Account opening

In response to your feedback, we've improved the opening process to make it as fluid as possible:

* No less than 16 improvements have been built to optimize your customers' Qonto account opening experience (reorganization of the onboarding path steps, new success screens, return to the partner app at the end of the opening...).
* You can now get more visibility on the KYC status of your customers who have opened a Qonto account, and thus know whether the application is accepted or still in progress (retrieval via this [endpoint](https://api-doc.qonto.com/docs/business-api/9b162ab362510-retrieve-the-authenticated-membership)).

## 🔐 SCA

Implementing Strong Customer *Authentication* (SCA) is essential for integrating Qonto and enabling your users to access their means of payment.

* From now on, thanks to SMS OTP (one-time password), your customers will be able to receive a security code by SMS as an alternative solution when the main strong authentication method doesn't work or isn't available.
* No need for an associated device or actual authentication to test strong authentication (SCA) in the sandbox. The development phase and your integration is considerably simplified.

## 💳 Cards

Our API-based functionality for ordering physical or virtual cards lets you offer payment methods while maintaining a fully integrated user experience.

We've added the following actions:

* Modify card parameters [(limits](https://api-doc.qonto.com/docs/business-api/c397cd040deb5-update-a-card-s-limits), [ATM withdrawals, online payments](https://api-doc.qonto.com/docs/business-api/55399b86dca4e-update-a-card-s-options))
* Temporarily block/unblock a card [(endpoint](https://api-doc.qonto.com/docs/business-api/28211040db636-lock-a-card) [lock/unlock](https://api-doc.qonto.com/docs/business-api/3bcff6ee72abf-unlock-a-card))
* Report a card as lost or stolen [(endpoint report as](https://api-doc.qonto.com/docs/business-api/b671103de465b-report-a-physical-card-as-lost) [lost/stolen](https://api-doc.qonto.com/docs/business-api/52d99366ef4a6-report-a-physical-card-as-stolen))
* Automatically create a virtual card for your customers when they open their Qonto account (*on request* → let us know 👋 )

All without leaving your app!

## **Beneficiary Webhooks: Real-Time Sync, Zero Polling**

Here's a problem we just solved: constantly checking if beneficiary data changed.

We just shipped Beneficiary Webhooks that eliminate that pain entirely. Now your systems get instant notifications whenever a beneficiary is created, updated, or deleted.

**What this unlocks:**

* Real-Time Synchronization - Get notified instantly when beneficiaries change. Your system reacts immediately instead of constantly asking "has anything changed?"
* Smarter Payment Automation - Automatically validate and sync beneficiary data before SEPA transfers or bulk payments. 
* Security Built In - Track changes to trusted beneficiaries (critical since they can bypass SCA for automated transfers). Maintain rock-solid audit trails of who added or modified payment recipients.

**The bottom line:**

Your beneficiary data is no longer a source of friction—it's a trigger for automation. When your systems know about changes the instant they happen, everything becomes more efficient, more secure, and more reliable.

# October
Source: https://docs.qonto.com/qonto-embed/product-updates/2025/october



## **Verification of Payee (VoP): a new era of secure transfers**

Starting October 9, 2025, European Regulation requires all banks and payment service providers to implement a game-changing security feature: Verification of Payee (VoP).

**What's the big deal?**

VoP is a mandatory extra layer of protection to every transfer by verifying in real-time that the beneficiary name matches the IBAN. No more silent errors, no more fraud surprises—just peace of mind.

As mandated for managing payment services, Qonto Embed partners must also integrate Verification of Payee to ensure their users benefit from this enhanced security layer.

**How it works:**

There are two different ways in which partners can start using transfers with VoP. 

* SEPA transfer creation flow: This flow is now available as a multi-step process you can trigger by calling an SDK function. It redirects your users to Qonto’s SEPA transfer creation flow and returns them to your website once completed. The flow includes the newly added Verification of Payee to further secure your users' transfers.
* VoP check in SDK SEPA transfers: If you don't need a UI flow, the SDK now also incorporates the VoP check in the create-transfer functions. 

**The best part?** It's completely free for your users and represents a major step forward in payment security.

## **Payment Link Webhooks: Stay in the loop, automatically**

**First, a quick reminder: What are Payment Links?**

Payment links are your users' secret weapon for getting paid faster. Your users can create a professional invoice, add a secure payment link with one click, and send it to clients. They pay using their preferred method—and you get paid 5x faster. Simple as that.

Now, we just made them even more powerful with **two brand-new webhooks** that keep you updated on everything happening with your payment links.

* Payment Link lifecycle webhook - Get notified every time a payment link changes status (open, processing, paid, canceled, expired).
* Feature Activation webhook - Get notified the moment your user activates the payment link feature. Perfect for triggering welcome flows or onboarding sequences.

![Payment links Qonto.png](https://mintcdn.com/qonto-6237c309/VoCbhg1r1cFoRSfY/images/PaymentlinksQonto.png?fit=max\&auto=format\&n=VoCbhg1r1cFoRSfY\&q=85\&s=77dbd615c9ea45a7c3bee2ac7b33c66a)

**Why this matters:**

No more guessing. No more manual checks. Your systems now stay perfectly in sync with payment link activity in real time. Automate workflows, trigger notifications, update dashboards—all without lifting a finger.

An SDK (Software Development Kit) is basically a **complete package of ready-to-use building blocks** - think libraries, code samples, UI components, and documentation - that developers can use instead of building everything from scratch.

Each new SDK feature we ship expands what's possible with Qonto Embed, giving our partners access to more powerful banking features.

# Qonto Embed
Source: https://docs.qonto.com/qonto-embed/qonto-embed



## **What is Qonto Embed?**

Qonto Embed is the ultimate solution for seamlessly integrating banking capabilities into any B2B SaaS platform. We strive to revolutionize the addition of banking features by providing a simple, yet powerful experience.

With Qonto Embed, you can enhance your platform with advanced payment features and financial tools, or introduce innovative functionalities to boost customer retention and increase your bottom line, all in just a few easy steps!

![image.png](https://mintcdn.com/qonto-6237c309/IZJ5MTjzXpp-Ln1p/QEbanner.png?fit=max\&auto=format\&n=IZJ5MTjzXpp-Ln1p\&q=85\&s=732829ac352becaf2e34d06dcfc41646)

## **Embedded Finance: A Strategic Lever for Growth**

In our rapidly evolving digital landscape, embedded finance emerges as a transformative force for platforms seeking strategic growth. By integrating financial services directly into their offerings, platforms can enhance user experiences, drive customer engagement, and unlock new revenue streams. The demand for embedded finance is clear; it not only addresses the financial service gaps faced by many businesses but also positions platforms as comprehensive solutions in their respective markets.

## **Key Considerations**

When deciding whether to invest into embedded finance solutions, there are three critical levers to consider:

### **1. Go to Market Speed**

Speed is of the essence in today's competitive environment. Building a payment solution from scratch can delay market entry, potentially missing out on valuable opportunities. A banking solution requires time, expertise, licenses, risk management and ultimately involves a lot of resources.

Blog post Qonto on CBS: [here](https://qonto.com/fr/blog/tendances/nouveautes/plateforme-bancaire-qonto)

Opting for an embedded finance provider allows for rapid integration, ensuring your platform remains agile and responsive to market demands.

With Qonto Embed, you can distribue payment services to manage many use cases via API with a 2-week GO-live or choose a minor level of integration (e.g. redirection link) for an even lighter and faster connection.

### **2. Cost Control**

Effective cost management is crucial for ensuring sustainable growth and maintaining a competitive advantage.

Developing in-house banking solutions requires significant upfront investments, not just in technology development, but also in acquiring necessary licenses, ensuring regulatory compliance, and assembling specialized teams with expertise in finance and technology. These costs can escalate quickly, particularly when accounting for ongoing operational expenses and the need to keep pace with rapidly evolving regulatory landscapes.

In contrast, partnering with an embedded finance provider offers a more financially prudent approach. By leveraging Qonto's established infrastructure and regulatory expertise, platforms can avoid these substantial initial outlays. This partnership enables businesses to allocate resources more strategically, focusing on core competencies and innovation rather than the complexities of financial service delivery.

With Qonto's transparent pricing (for SMBs) and Qonto Embed's “profit-only\*” revenue-sharing model (for you), platforms gain better financial predictability and can more accurately forecast their economic outcomes, ensuring that every investment contributes directly to growth and profitability.

*\*Qonto does not bill any (setup, monthly, transaction, operation) fees to Qonto Embed partners.*

### **3. Revenue and Monetization Lever: Unlock Your Potential**

Embedded finance not only enhances your platform’s capabilities but also unlocks new avenues for revenue. By leveraging advanced monetization strategies, platforms can maximize customer value and generate faster returns on investment, providing a competitive edge in the marketplace.

With Qonto Embed, you not only launch a business account: you make sure the launch is a success by relying on the product and brand leader in the European market.

## **Navigating Embedded Finance: Why Qonto Embed is Your Strategic Advantage**

Qonto Embed stands out as the ideal choice for platforms aiming to integrate embedded finance:

* **Proven Technology and Track Record**: Qonto’s in-house technology has successfully served over 500,000 SMEs across Europe, demonstrating a robust and reliable infrastructure that supports growth and innovation.
* **Brand and Marketing Firepower**: As a leading financial services brand, Qonto provides significant marketing capabilities that enhance your platform's visibility and credibility, attracting and retaining more customers.
* **Exceptional Customer Success**: With a Net Promoter Score (NPS) of +75, Qonto is committed to delivering outstanding customer experiences. This focus on customer success ensures satisfaction and loyalty, crucial for long-term growth.
* **Innovative Revenue Model**: Qonto Embed's revenue model is designed for profitability, with no upfront costs and revenue sharing. This approach ensures that your platform reaps the financial benefits without the burden of additional expenses.

## **Conclusion: Partner with Qonto Embed for Success and Unlock Revenues**

Incorporating embedded finance through Qonto Embed offers platforms a strategic advantage. By leveraging Qonto’s technology, brand strength, customer success, and innovative revenue model, your platform can enhance its offerings, driving growth and profitability. Partner with Qonto Embed to unlock new revenue opportunities and position your business for long-term success.

Explore Qonto Embed today and transform your platform into a comprehensive financial solution that meets the evolving needs of your users.

# Card management
Source: https://docs.qonto.com/qonto-embed/use-cases/card-management



### **Introduction to Card Management API**

Implementing our API for card management, provides significant advantages. It allows businesses to automate and streamline their card-related operations, enhancing efficiency, security, and user experience. This can be particularly beneficial for companies with multiple employees needing access to company funds or for those who prioritize tight financial control and security.

### **Advantages of Qonto's Card API**

1. **Automation**: Automates card issuance, activation, and management processes, reducing manual workload.
2. **Security**: Provides robust security features like locking/unlocking cards and reporting lost/stolen cards, ensuring financial safety.
3. **Control**: Allows setting limits and restrictions on cards, offering detailed control over expenditures.
4. **Flexibility**: Supports both physical and virtual cards, catering to diverse business needs.
5. **Integration**: Seamless integration with existing financial tools and systems through OAuth 2.0.

### **Use Cases and Technical Implementation**

### **1. Create Cards**

* **Endpoint**: [/v2/cards](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/create-a-card)
  * **Use Case**: Automate the issuance of new cards for employees.
  * **Requirements:** Ensure all required fields such as type, holder, and initial limits are correctly filled. As creating a card is a sensitive this endpoint requires [SCA](/api-reference/business-api/authentication/sca/sca-flows).

### **1.1 Create cards in bulk**

* **Endpoint:** [/v2/cards\_bulk](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/bulk-create-cards)
  * **Use Case**: Automate card issuance for teams and departments.
  * **Requirements:** Ensure all required fields such as type, holder, and initial limits are correctly filled.

### **2. List Cards**

* **Endpoint**: [/v2/cards](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/list-cards)
* **Use Case**: Retrieve a list of all cards under an organization for auditing or management purposes.

### **3. Retrieve a Card iFrame URL**

* **Documentation**: [cards iframe SDK](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/sdk-libraries/variables/cards)
* **Use Case**: Securely display card details or management options within an application. Use the GET method to retrieve a URL that can be embedded securely within your application.

### **4. Manage Your Card Lifecycle**

**Report a Card as Lost or Stolen**

* **Endpoints**:
  * [/v2/cards//lost](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/report-a-physical-card-as-lost)
  * [/v2/cards//stolen](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/report-a-physical-card-as-stolen)
* **Use Case**: Immediately deactivate cards that are lost or stolen to prevent unauthorized transactions.

**Discard a Card**

* **Endpoint**: [/v2/cards//discard](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/discard-a-card)
* **Use Case**: Permanently deactivate unused or compromised cards.

**Lock and Unlock a Card**

* **Endpoints**:
  * [/v2/cards//lock](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/lock-a-card)
  * [/v2/cards//unlock](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/unlock-a-card)
* **Use Case**: Temporarily disable cards during suspicious activity or when not in use. Unlocking a card is a sensitive this endpoint requires [SCA](/api-reference/business-api/authentication/sca/sca-flows).

### **5. Change Card Settings**

**Update Card Limits and Restrictions**

* **Endpoints**:
  * [/v2/cards//limits](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/update-a-cards-limits) for updating spending limits. [SCA](/api-reference/business-api/authentication/sca/sca-flows) is required for this endpoint.
  * [/v2/cards//restrictions](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/update-a-cards-restrictions) for setting usage restrictions such as geographical or vendor-specific limits.
* **Use Case**: Manage and adjust spending policies dynamically to align with business needs.

**Update Card Options and Nickname**

* **Endpoints**:
  * [/v2/cards//options](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/update-a-cards-options) for general card settings.
  * [/v2/cards//nickname](https://docs.qonto.com/api-reference/business-api/cards/update-a-cards-nickname) to personalize card identification.
* **Use Case**: Customize card settings to enhance usability and identification within the organization.

# Adding payments
Source: https://docs.qonto.com/qonto-embed/use-cases/payments



One of the most straightforward and implemented use case of our Business API is adding Payment capabilities within a partner’s app.

If you already help your customers manage their invoices, automating your payments can help with 2 use cases:

💸 optimize providers bills payment thanks to bulk payouts (up to 400 bills) in 1 click

✨ reduce payment delays and increase recoveries success rate by sharing a payment link with clients bills

\--\
\
Let’s focus on *💸 Optimize providers bills payment*

If you already show your customers the invoices they’ll need to pay within your interface, the one thing missing is a “Pay” button which will allow to pay up to 400 bills in just 1 click from their Qonto account

The magic behind hitting that “Pay” button involves:

1. Retrieving the transfers beneficiaries’ data
2. Setting up SCA
3. Creating the transfers

Below the key steps to consider for a smooth implementation:

## 1. Retrieve the transfers Beneficiaries’ Data

### a. Finding existing beneficiaries’ data

**Objective:**

* Determine if the beneficiary is already trusted or if new details need to be added.

**Endpoints**

* [****GET /v2/sepa/beneficiaries:****](/api-reference/business-api/payments-transfers/sepa-transfers/beneficiaries/sepa-beneficiaries/index) use this endpoint in order to retrieve the list of beneficiaries for the authenticated organization.

If the beneficiary is not trusted on Qonto, you can add it via API, but your customers will need trust it themselves directly from the app → [https://support-fr.qonto.com/hc/en-us/articles/23947644174993-How-can-I-mark-a-payee-as-trustworthy#h\_925061f25d](https://support-fr.qonto.com/hc/en-us/articles/23947644174993-How-can-I-mark-a-payee-as-trustworthy#h_925061f25d)

### b. Add Beneficiaries if necessary (coming soon)

## 2. Set up SCA (Strong Customer Authentication)

**Objective:**

* Secure the transaction to ensure it is authorized by the account holder or administrators

**Steps:**

* **Choose Authentication Method**:
  * **Trusted Device**: User will receive a push notification on their registered device (computer or mobile) to approve the transaction.
  * **Passkeys**: User will receive a text message with a link to approve or set up a passkey.

**Initiate SCA**:

* **Specify Method**: Use the `X-Qonto-2fa-Preference` header in your request to choose between `paired-device` or `passkey`.
* **Start SCA Process**: When initiating a sensitive transaction, provide this header to indicate the preferred method of authentication.

**Endpoints for SCA:**

* **GET** [/v2/sca\_sessions/\<sca-session-token> ](/api-reference/business-api/authentication/sca/sca-flows): Poll this endpoint to check the status of the SCA session. The session will return one of three outcomes:
  * **waiting**: User has not yet approved the action.
  * **deny**: User has refused the action or the session expired (after 15 minutes).
  * **allow**: User has authorized the action.

**Functional Details**:

* Ensure the user is aware of the pending authorization and guide them on how to approve the transaction.
* Handle scenarios where the user may not receive the notification or delays in response.

**Example Headers for Authorization:**

```json theme={null}
{
  "X-Qonto-2fa-Preference": "paired-device",
  "X-Qonto-Sca-Session-Token": "<sca-session-token>"
}
```

## 3. Create Transfers

**Objective:**

* Execute the transfer, either to a trusted beneficiary or with new beneficiary data.

**Case 1: Create a transfer for a beneficiary**

Creates a single SEPA transfer for a given beneficiary.

**Endpoint:**

* POST [/v2/sepa/transfers](/api-reference/business-api/payments-transfers/sepa-transfers/sepa-transfers/create)

**Example Request:**

```json theme={null}
{
  "vop_proof_token": "proof_1234567890abcdef",
  "transfer": {
    "bank_account_id": "0a8df251-de2a-4394-bffc-6b9d9795700d",
    "beneficiary_id": "3e4d5f6a-7b8c-9d0e-1f2a-3b4c5d6e7f8a",
    "reference": "string",
    "note": "string",
    "scheduled_date": "2021-07-12",
    "amount": "string",
    "attachment_ids": [
      "497f6eca-6276-4993-bfeb-53cbbbba6f08"
    ]
  }
}
```

**Functional Details**:

* Verify that the `beneficiary_id` is correct and corresponds to the intended recipient. Alternatively, provide an inline `beneficiary` object with `name` and `iban`.
* Ensure that the amount is correctly formatted and within transaction limits.
* This endpoint requires SCA unless the beneficiary is trusted.
* For transfers exceeding €30,000, at least one attachment is required.

**Case 2: Create a SEPA bulk transfer**

Create up to 400 transfers at once. This action requires human validation before processing.

If the person that initiates the transfers is neither owner nor administrator of the account, they can prepare the transfers and then send a request so that the owner or administrator can validate via SCA - please look into the roles that can be set in Qonto → [https://support-fr.qonto.com/hc/en-us/articles/23947722927249-What-are-the-different-roles-available](https://support-fr.qonto.com/hc/en-us/articles/23947722927249-What-are-the-different-roles-available)

**Endpoint:**

* POST [/v2/sepa/bulk\_transfers](/api-reference/business-api/payments-transfers/sepa-transfers/sepa-bulk-transfers/create)

**Example Request:**

```json theme={null}
{
  "vop_proof_token": "proof_1234567890abcdef",
  "bank_account_id": "0a8df251-de2a-4394-bffc-6b9d9795700d",
  "bulk_transfers": [
    {
      "beneficiary_id": "3e4d5f6a-7b8c-9d0e-1f2a-3b4c5d6e7f8a",
      "client_transfer_id": "0cd90e5a-2c03-4ab6-81a8-d48818026e58",
      "reference": "string",
      "note": "string",
      "scheduled_date": "2021-07-12",
      "amount": "string",
      "attachment_ids": [
        "497f6eca-6276-4993-bfeb-53cbbbba6f08"
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

**Functional Details**:

* Ensure that you are SCA-enrolled.
* Double-check all beneficiary details to prevent errors in the transaction.
* Confirm the transaction details with the user before submission to ensure accuracy and prevent unauthorized transfers.

**General Functional Considerations**:

* Log all transaction attempts with timestamps and status for audit and troubleshooting purposes.
* Solo basic plans are not allowed to do bulk transfers. If a bulk transfer is attempted, it will fail and an error will be returned.
* For the rest of the plans, you can create up to 400 SEPA transfers at once.
* Be reminded that the number of monthly transfers included in the customer's Qonto plan is listed [here](https://legal.qonto.com/fr?_gl=1*147ftf2*_gcl_aw*R0NMLjE3Mzg3NTIwNTkuQ2p3S0NBaUF0WXk5QmhCY0Vpd0FOV1FRTDRJbWJLeDhYVkFkc2gyek13cEtrRm5JVEw4T0JIdGI3WFlSVDF2WFd2dGVHVlhGamcxVWp4b0NyY1VRQXZEX0J3RQ.._gcl_auOTY5NTg3MDE1LjE3Mzc1NTY3MDQ.#template-k0dnmvpv0).
* This endpoint requires user interaction for approval of transfers.

# Security measures
Source: https://docs.qonto.com/security-and-compliance/security-measures

Qonto SA operates the Qonto application along with its supporting infrastructure which allows professional clients to open a payment account and execute payment transactions.

## Context

These operations are available through multiple platforms and third-party-integrations maintained by Qonto SA for the sake of their customers:

* Web application available at [https://app.qonto.com](https://app.qonto.com/)
* Android and iOS applications available respectively in the Play and Apple stores.
* PSD2-compatible API available at [https://thirdparty.qonto.com](https://thirdparty.qonto.com/)

Each of these public entry-points further communicates with a number of backend HTTP endpoints in order to perform specific business functions.

## Multi-factor authentication

Clients are required to choose a password that respects the following constraints:

* 9 characters minimum
* Complex enough to pass an algorithmic strength test

These rules are in accordance with the NIST 800-63b [directive](https://pages.nist.gov/800-63-3/sp800-63b.html).

Furthermore and in compliance with PSD2, customers are required to set up a second authentication factor in order to approve sensitive actions (login, payments and so on). Each validation screen on the phone contains enough context (e.g., IP address in the case of Login or beneficiary IBAN in the case of transfer) to allow customers to make an informed decision and detect any suspicious activity.

## Web application firewall

All of Qonto’s public endpoints are protected behind a Web Application Firewall (WAF), that provides the following security measures:

* Rejection of malicious requests
* Blockage of requests exploiting Web application vulnerabilities
* Blockage of requests issued from suspicious IP addresses
* DDOS protection

## Encryption and data confidentiality

Communication between customers and the Qonto application are encrypted using publicly trusted TLS certificates supporting strong encryption and hashing algorithms.

## Server and application hardening

We automatically update all of our software, packages and servers to guarantee the security of the components running the Qonto application.

Servers are configured to only contain the packages required to run our applications therefore minimising the attack surface. We follow all the hardening guidelines provided by the international standards such as NIST 800-123.

## Security assessments

Every code change submitted to the Qonto application is scanned for vulnerabilities and malicious packages. The code is also submitted a human review and must pass non-regression tests before deployment.

Qonto calls on a specialised third party auditor to perform annual security assessment. The result of this assessment is shared with the Executive team.

## Backup and resiliency

Customer data is backed up in AWS S3 along with the guarantees that this service provides in terms of [durability](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/DataDurability.html).

The Qonto application is actively running in three AWS datacenters around the Paris region insuring that no single point of failure could impact our quality of service.

We perform an annual business continuity plan where we simulate in real conditions the failure of a critical component and stress test our technical mitigations and process.

## Qonto’s internal security

On top of the protections mentioned above, Qonto employs many more security measures to protect the infrastructure against various threats:

* Phishing-resistant multi-factor authentication (WebAuthn) is mandatory for all employees
* Fine-grained access control coupled with the least privilege principle
* Detection of suspicious behaviour covering all our critical tools
* An infrastructure described as code to easily identify all assets
* Ephemeral access
* Encryption of all datastores

## Security certifications

As a payment institution authorised and supervised by the ACPR, Qonto is subject to many regulatory requirements mandating strong security commitments:

* [DIRECTIVE (EU) 2015/2366 on Payment Services](https://eur-lex.europa.eu/legal-content/EN/TXT/HTML/?uri=CELEX:32015L2366\&from=EN#d1e1059-35-1) (DSP2)
* [EBA Guidelines on ICT and Security Risk Management](https://eba.europa.eu/sites/default/documents/files/documents/10180/2522896/32a28233-12f5-49c8-9bb5-f8744ccb4e92/Final%20Guidelines%20on%20ICT%20and%20security%20risk%20management.pdf)
* [“arrêté du 3 novembre 2014”](https://www.legifrance.gouv.fr/loda/article_lc/LEGIARTI000043224871) as well as the [IT risk notice](https://acpr.banque-france.fr/sites/default/files/media/2021/07/08/20210707_notice_risque_it.pdf) ACPR
* [Digital Operational Resilience Act](https://eur-lex.europa.eu/legal-content/EN/TXT/PDF/?uri=CELEX:32022R2554)
* Compliance with PCI-DSS requirements

Qonto was certified ISO 27001 in July 2023 on the e-invoicing scope.

## Third party access through the open banking API

In compliance with PSD2 requirements, Qonto is mandated to provide an open banking interface to licensed entities (aggregators, accounting software, and so on). Customers can access their Qonto data through licenced payment services providers thanks to the integration of this interface.

Each partner may request one or several scopes to make their integration work: access to the balance, transactions, attachments and so on. Their detailed permissions are presented to the customer upon installation of the integration. See the screen below for an example:

![](https://qonto-assets.s3.eu-central-1.amazonaws.com/oauth-clients/OAuth+2.0+consent.png)

Each integration may establish its own frequency of access and data synchronisation to suit their provided service.

Availability and response-time statistics for this interface are published each quarter on [Qonto Developers](https://developers.qonto.com/api-performance/).
